# PROOF-2a: the plan, as a structured proof

*Beside `PROOF-2a-THEOREM-draft-2026-09-24.md`, which states the theorem. This file is the plan to reach the state in which the theorem can be witnessed, written in Lamport's structured style: numbered steps, each a claim with a checkable acceptance, its dependencies, and its dispatch log. Every dispatch is entered under its step before it goes out; every reviewed return updates the current table and the step's log line. Joe, 2026-09-26: the proof is to be understandable from this summary, and dispatches are to be logged into it, not to occur reactively.*

**Conventions.** `⟨n⟩m` is step m at depth n. `ACCEPT:` a predicate someone can check from the record. `DEPENDS:` steps that must be ACCEPTED first. `PAR:` what the step parallelises over, and which seats may take it (Claude seats for map and prover work; Kimi seats, one unique task each via `futon3c/scripts/kimi-task.sh` with a test-registry or `lake build` warrant as acceptance, for per-row, per-edge and per-lane work). `STATUS:` refers to the current table. Completion is Completed or Not completed; Activity is Active only for a currently running named job. The older PENDING / IN FLIGHT / ACCEPTED wording in LOGs is historical. `LOG:` packet · seat · job · outcome · shas.

**THEOREM** (PROOF-2a, unchanged): the machine, on a lifecycle-shaped mission chosen by its own selection (T), constructs an ordinary, semantically nontrivial field (0), enacts the chosen candidate (C), and satisfies clauses 1–6 on the resulting record.

**PREMISE, corrected 2026-09-26.** The plan had assumed the components were ready and only needed wiring. The wiring map (M-wm-wiring), its adjacency matrix, the registry refreshed to this proof's equations, the Lean dependency map, and the typeset Figure 6A together show otherwise: equations exist as prose, dependencies the registry asserts are not imported in Lean, and no wire past selection has ever carried a value live. Joe's order: components, then wires, then the empirical questions. No flight runs before ⟨1⟩ and ⟨2⟩ are ACCEPTED.

The equation figures refresh independently every 30 seconds through proof-2a-figures.timer (p4ng 3e2edeb), keyed by committed input contents and renderer/toolchain identity; unchanged inputs reuse cached outputs. Uncommitted edits, validation failures and overdue checks are visible on the checkpoint. The page publisher does not regenerate or certify the other figures.

### ⟨1⟩2. Every `:latex-absent` row is either written in Lean or ruled not an equation.
ACCEPT: for each row from ⟨1⟩1, either a Lean declaration exists that the row's `:lean` names and `gen_lean_dag.bb` places (with a `lake build` warrant), or the row is reclassified to `:plumbing` with the reason (as the grain gate was: a support restriction, not an equation). Expected rows: `:mission-preference` (C's reading rule), `:enactment-habit` (E's counting rule), `:target-universe`, plus whatever ⟨2⟩1a classes as not-written.
DEPENDS: ⟨1⟩1, ⟨2⟩1a. PAR: per row; Kimi-able (one E-kimi-task per row, `lake build` warrant), Lean layer reviewed by claude-8. Also: at HEAD the decision's measured A is ALWAYS the typed absence because no labels reach it (claude-12's seam). Continuity: each click re-initialises sPrev from fresh target facts, so step i+1's sPrev must be the chain's own q_i, only step 1 taking the run record's belief. Unmeasured A: SPEC-F requires measured A for a step to be admitted; a step with an unmeasured class is refused and the admitted prefix ends there; the policy's F is then `:computed` over the earlier steps or `:not-supplied` if none; `:zero-support` only for P(o) = 0. ## Order of execution

The current table names the remaining work and the snapshot's active packets; the LOGs retain job ids and reviews. Do not dispatch from the old afternoon queues quoted in those logs. Finish the active component and wire packets; reconcile the registry, figures and map at explicit pins; complete the open component / preference obligations and both wire-test layers. Then establish ⟨2⟩5, then run ⟨3⟩. A packet return does not itself authorise the next flight.

## Generation rule (added 14:29Z, after two incidents of concurrent regeneration in the shared p4ng tree)
A generator is run only from committed inputs (`AIF_EQ`/`git show <rev>:` for the registry and the map) and only after `git status` shows the generator itself committed; outputs are committed by the same lane in the commit that follows the generator change (a generator must be committed before it runs, so when the generator changes the two cannot share a commit: R1's order, generator with the map then the outputs, is the pattern; clarified 15:00Z on claude-10's question); one lane regenerates a given output set at a time, and a packet that must regenerate says which set it takes. A restamp that changes anything but the stamp is a defect to correct in a follow-up commit, as ab924198 did.

**Automatic equation figures (Joe, 2026-09-26):** the live checkpoint figures now follow committed input contents automatically through p4ng `refresh_aif.py`. Their output sets and provenance receipts are immutable content-keyed releases in the web root, selected by one atomic status file; they are not automatically committed into a shared source tree. The generation rule above continues to govern manually maintained historical outputs and other figure lanes. Committed generator/input snapshots, cache validation and explicit failure/freshness reporting apply to the automatic path too.
